## Codebase Patterns

### Server File Pattern
Every `+page.server.ts` follows:
1. Import: `connectDB`, models, `requirePermission`
2. Guard: `requirePermission(locals.user, 'domain:read|write')`
3. Connect: `await connectDB()`
4. Query: Mongoose `.find()`, `.findById()`, `.aggregate()` with `.lean()`
5. Serialize: `JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(result))`
6. Return: exact shape from CONTRACT-REGISTRY.md

### Auth Pattern
- `event.locals.user` — authenticated user (set by hooks.server.ts)
- `event.locals.session` — session object
- `requirePermission(locals.user, 'permission:string')` — throws error(403) if missing
- `hasPermission(user, 'perm')` — boolean check (used in layouts for UI flags)
- `isAdmin(user)` — defined but currently unused
- Cookie: `auth-session`, SHA-256 hashed, looked up in `sessions` MongoDB collection
- Auth library: custom implementation on `@oslojs/crypto` + `@oslojs/encoding` (Lucia was removed in the Postgres→MongoDB migration)

### ID Generation
```typescript
import { generateId } from '$lib/server/db/models';
const id = generateId(); // nanoid string
```

### Denormalization Pattern
At write time, embed operator/user/project info:
```typescript
createdBy: { _id: user._id, username: user.username }
project: { _id: project._id, name: project.name, color: project.color }
```

### Sacred Document Pattern
- Check `finalizedAt` before any mutation
- Use `corrections[]` array for post-finalization fixes
- Write-once phases on CartridgeRecord: check `phase.recordedAt: { $exists: false }`

### Immutable Log Pattern
- AuditLog, ElectronicSignature, InventoryTransaction, DeviceEvent, ManufacturingMaterialTransaction
- Append only — never update or delete
- Mongoose middleware blocks `.updateOne()`, `.deleteOne()`, etc.

### API Auth Pattern (Agent API)
```typescript
// Centralized: src/lib/server/api-auth.ts (timing-safe comparison)
import { requireAgentApiKey } from '$lib/server/api-auth';
// Note: 9 endpoints still use local inline requireApiKey() — consolidation needed
```

### Deployment Rule (MANDATORY)
**NEVER deploy to Vercel from local.** Always:
1. Commit to the feature branch
2. `git push origin <branch>` to GitHub
3. Deploy from GitHub via Vercel (linked to the repo)
No `npx vercel` from local working directory. All deployments must be traceable to a GitHub commit.

## Phase 8: Database Cleanup (Mar 23)

### DB-01: Ghost Collection Removal — executed on master
- **Audit:** 164 collections found, 53 active models. 111 ghost collections from Postgres migration.

- **Tier 2 executed:** Migrated 119 documents from 15 camelCase twins into canonical snake_case collections, then dropped the camelCase sources. Zero duplicates, all verified.
- **Result:** 164 → 61 collections.
- **Left for team discussion:** `assaydefinitions` (3 docs, divergent schema from `assay_definitions`), `receiving_lots` vs `backing_lots` disambiguation, seed script fix.

## Phase 11: Mongoose Subdocument ObjectId Fix (Mar 24, 2026)

### Problem
New users created through the admin UI got 500 errors on every page load. Root cause: Mongoose auto-generates ObjectId `_id` on subdocument arrays unless explicitly disabled with `{ _id: false }`. The `user.roles[]` subdocument was missing this flag. SvelteKit cannot serialize ObjectId objects.

This only affected users whose roles were assigned through Mongoose (admin UI). Users created via raw MongoDB driver (seed scripts) were unaffected because the driver doesn't add auto-generated `_id` fields.

### Fixes applied
1. **Schema fixes** — Added `_id: false` to 11 subdocument arrays across 8 model files:
   - `user.ts`: roles, communicationPreferences
   - `bom-item.ts`: versionHistory
   - `cartridge-record.ts`: tubeRecords, parts, spectroReadings
   - `manufacturing-settings.ts`: rejectionReasonCodes
   - `opentrons-robot.ts`: recentHealthSnapshots
   - `reagent-batch-record.ts`: tubeRecords, cartridgesFilled
   - `shipping-package.ts`: cartridges
   - `test-result.ts`: readings

2. **Layout serialization** — All 8 layouts that return `locals.user` now wrap with `JSON.parse(JSON.stringify())` as defense-in-depth

3. **Data cleanup** — Removed existing ObjectId `_id` from 2 affected user accounts via `scripts/fix-role-objectids.ts`

4. **Documentation** — CLAUDE.md updated with `_id: false` pitfall

### Impact assessment
- The 11 schema fixes are preventative — most load functions already manually map fields, so the ObjectId never reached the client
- The layout serialization was the actual fix for the 500 error — layouts pass the full user object unfiltered
- The larger codebase was not affected

## Known Issues (updated Mar 24, 2026)

### RESOLVED (this session)
- ~~50 page load functions missing `requirePermission()` checks~~ — SEC-01 through SEC-09
- ~~Manufacturing layout try/catch silently swallows permission errors~~ — SEC-01-01
- ~~Equipment deck/tray mutations completely unprotected~~ — SEC-05-02
- ~~Permission string typo: `testResults:read/write`~~ — SEC-01-02
- ~~4 files hardcode `roleName === 'admin'`~~ — SEC-01-03
- ~~9 API endpoints duplicate `requireApiKey()`~~ — SEC-01-04
- ~~`api/box/debug` endpoint has zero auth~~ — SEC-09-01 (deleted)
- ~~Mongoose subdocument ObjectId causes 500~~ — _id:false on 11 arrays + layout serialization
- ~~User roles scrambled by AI agent~~ — fix-user-roles.ts + assignRole $set fix

### Security — High
- **Deactivated users retain session access** — `validateSessionToken` doesn't check `isActive`. A deactivated user keeps full access for up to 30 days until session expires.
- **No audit logging on admin mutations** — createUser, updateProfile, deactivateUser, reactivateUser, resetPassword, assignRole, removeRole, createRole, updateRole, deleteRole, setPermissions — zero AuditLog entries. Most sensitive area of the app.
- **No login rate limiting** — unlimited brute force attempts, no lockout, no backoff

### Security — Medium
- **`removeRole` only revokes first roleHistory entry** — uses `$` positional operator instead of `arrayFilters`
- **`deleteRole` doesn't stamp roleHistory revocation** — audit trail shows grant but no revocation
- **Login error leaks server internals** — `fail(500, { error: \`Server error: ${message}\` })`
- **No password strength validation** — createUser, resetPassword, invite accept all allow single-character passwords

### Data / Schema
- `assaydefinitions` vs `assay_definitions` schema divergence unresolved (Monday team discussion)
- `scripts/seed-domain-data.ts` writes to wrong (camelCase) collection names
- `unitCost` stored as String in BOM/Part models, expected as Number
- `currentRevision`/`revision` stored as String, expected as Number
- `_id` vs `id` transformation inconsistent across layouts
- 136+ `as any` type assertions across server files

### Low / Deferred
- `svelte-check` reports 834 errors (pre-existing from copied UI layer)
- Box.com integration stubbed
- Opentrons endpoints depend on live robot hardware
- Seed script only creates users/roles (no domain test data via Mongoose)
- `isAdmin()` defined but never used
- Sacred document correction endpoints don't exist yet (will need `admin:full` when built)

## Phase 13b: Device Logging Pipeline (Mar 27–30, 2026)

### Overview
Built the BIMS side of the firmware device logging pipeline. Firmware v74 uploads session logs via Particle Cloud → AWS Lambda middleware → BIMS API. The Lambda parses firmware log text, extracts checkpoint/crash data, and POSTs structured data to BIMS endpoints. BIMS owns all writes (nanoid IDs, immutable middleware, Mongoose validation).

### New Models (Tier 3 — Immutable)
- **DeviceLog** (`device_logs`) — firmware session log uploads, 30-day TTL, unique sessionId index
- **DeviceCrash** (`device_crashes`) — crash reports from checkpoint analysis, NO TTL (kept forever)
- **WebhookLog** (`webhook_logs`) — Lambda request/response round-trips, 30-day TTL

### Modified Model
- **DeviceEvent** (`device_events`) — removed enum restriction on `eventType` (was `['validate', 'load_assay', 'upload', 'reset', 'error']`, now open String). Added 30-day TTL index on `createdAt`.

### API Endpoints (4 new, all under `/api/device/`)
- `POST /api/device/logs` — session log ingestion (API key auth)
- `POST /api/device/crashes` + `GET` — crash ingestion (API key) + dashboard queries (session auth, `device:read`)
- `POST /api/device/webhook-logs` — webhook log ingestion (API key auth)
- `POST /api/device/events` — catch-all Particle event archive (API key auth)

### Shared Utility
- `src/lib/checkpoint-codes.ts` — firmware checkpoint code → human-readable name mapping, crash category classification, `isInterruptedCheckpoint()` helper

### Dashboard Pages
- `/spu/[spuId]/diagnostics` — per-device diagnostics (linked from SPU detail page via "View Diagnostics" button, only shows when Particle device is linked)
- `/admin/device-crashes` — fleet-wide crash dashboard with aggregation stats, multi-filter, paginated table

### Unified Timeline View (primary diagnostics view)
Stitches all 4 data sources into one chronological feed per device:
- **Firmware log lines** — extracted from `device_logs.logLines[]`, each line positioned at wall-clock time
- **Webhook round-trips** — from `webhook_logs`, expandable request/response
- **Crash reports** — from `device_crashes`, expandable checkpoint sequence trail
- **Raw events** — from `device_events`, muted styling

### Key Technical Learnings

**Timestamp computation for firmware lines:**
The firmware `ms` values are millis since boot. The Lambda's `bootTime` field is unreliable — it falls back to `new Date()` (upload time) when it can't parse a boot time from the log text. Using `bootTime + ms` causes firmware lines to appear 20+ minutes in the future.

**Correct formula:** `wallClock = uploadedAt - maxMsInChunk + line.ms`. This anchors the last line of each upload chunk at `uploadedAt` and works backwards. Firmware lines then correctly interleave with webhook/event entries that use server wall-clock time.

**Upload chunks vs sessions:**
The device uploads every ~60 seconds during idle. Each upload chunk gets metadata headers prepended (`======== UPLOAD METADATA ========`, `Boot #N | Device:...`, `Firmware: vN | Format: vN`). These are NOT separate boot sessions — multiple chunks share the same `bootCount`. The BIMS groups uploads by `bootCount` and only shows a `SESSION START` boundary when `bootCount` changes (actual reboot).

**Metadata line filtering:**
Upload metadata lines (UPLOAD METADATA, `====` separators, `Boot #N |`, `Firmware: vN |`) are filtered from the timeline and session log views. The middleware already extracts `firmwareVersion` and `bootCount` as top-level document fields, so data isn't lost.

**Clock skew between sources:**
Firmware `ms` timestamps are from the device's internal clock; webhook/event timestamps are from Lambda/Particle Cloud servers. There can be 1-3 seconds of skew. This doesn't affect debugging — the relative ordering within each source is always correct. The UI shows both the wall-clock time and the firmware `ms` offset for each entry.

### PRD & Reference Docs
- `docs/logging-middleware-update/BIMS-LOGGING-PRD.md` — full implementation spec
- `docs/logging-middleware-update/CHANGES.md` — Lambda middleware changes
- `docs/logging-middleware-update/index.mjs` — updated Lambda handler (reference copy)
- `docs/logging-middleware-update/db-adapter.mjs` — updated db adapter with BIMS API forwarding (reference copy)
- `docs/BIMS-UI-REQUIREMENTS.md` — UI data display requirements with sample documents
- `docs/BIMS-UNIFIED-TIMELINE-SPEC.md` — unified timeline view spec

### Utility Scripts
- `scripts/cleanup-device-logs.ts` — wipes all logging data for device 219 (0a10aced202194944a0713b4) from all 4 collections. Run via `npx tsx scripts/cleanup-device-logs.ts`.

### Deployment Order (for future reference)
1. Deploy BIMS API endpoints first (this work)
2. Set `BIMS_API_URL` and `BIMS_API_KEY` env vars on Lambda
3. Deploy updated Lambda (`index.mjs` + `db-adapter.mjs`)
4. Create Particle webhook for `device-log` events
5. Deploy firmware with session log upload capability

# 2026-04-22 → 2026-04-23 — Cartridge/Backing-Lot Refactor + Manufacturing Analytics

## Starting problem (2026-04-22)

Dashboard was lying: Oven 1 showed 0 cartridges despite physically holding 83.
"Backing cartridges" pipeline count stuck too high. Wax QC Yield stuck at 0%.

## Root causes discovered

1. **Oven tile hardcoded `count: 0`** in cartridge-dashboard/+page.server.ts:146-151.
2. **BackingLot.cartridgeCount never decremented** — set once at WI-01 time, stayed
   stale forever regardless of how many cartridges wax-filling pulled.
3. **Two parallel CartridgeRecord pools** that never reconciled:
   - WI-01 → nanoid-ID placeholders w/ `status='backing'` that were never consumed
   - Wax-filling `loadDeck` → UUID records from scanned barcodes (different IDs)
   Result: lineage fragmented, ghost counts inflated forever.
4. **waxQc.status='Accepted' was never written** on the happy path — only the
   reject path wrote `'Rejected'`. So "Wax QC Yield" KPI = 0 numerator.

## Initial fixes (2026-04-22)

Code changes:
- `cartridge-dashboard/+page.server.ts`: oven tile now queries BackingLot sum
- `wax-filling/+page.server.ts` `loadDeck`: decrements BackingLot.cartridgeCount,
  marks `consumed` at zero, voids stale WI-01 placeholders on consumption
- `wax-filling` + `opentron-control/wax/[runId]` `completeQC`: writes
  `waxQc.status='Accepted'` for every non-rejected cart
- `equipment/activity`, `equipment/location/[locationId]`, `cartridge-admin/storage`:
  status filters + BackingLot-based oven counts
- `manufacturing/consumables/+page.server.ts`: `backedCount` reads BackingLot sum

Backfills applied:
- 9,641 historical cartridges → `waxQc.status='Accepted'` (system-backfill tag)
- 2 BackingLots → `status='consumed'`, `cartridgeCount=0`
- 56 + 2 WI-01 nanoid placeholders voided
- 27 final nanoid stubs deleted

## Major redesign (2026-04-22 evening)

User clarified intent: cartridges should only individuate when their barcode is
FIRST SCANNED at wax deck loading. WI-01 should track raw-material lots and
aggregate bucket counts, nothing per-cartridge.

Refactor:
- WI-01 no longer creates CartridgeRecord docs; only LotRecord + BackingLot
- `loadDeck` resolves parent LotRecord via bucketBarcode and copies material
  lineage (cartridgeBlankLot, thermosealLot, barcodeLabelLot, qrCodeRef,
  ovenEntryTime) onto each scanned cartridge
- CartridgeRecord schema extended: parentLotRecordId, lotQrCode, cartridgeBlankLot,
  thermosealLot, barcodeLabelLot, ovenExitTime added to `backing` subdoc
- Dashboards + oven tiles all use `BackingLot.cartridgeCount` as source of
  truth instead of counting CartridgeRecords
- Reagent-filling: removed defensive `$setOnInsert: status='backing'` orphan path

Commit `97082dc` on dev → merged to master → main (deployed).

## Thorough audit via Opus 4.7 subagent

Full Mongo verification. Key findings:
- BLOCKER-1: refactor committed but not yet deployed at time of audit. 98 today's
  cartridges were missing the new lineage fields (fixed via follow-up backfill).
- BLOCKER-2: lot `2941bb67` over-pulled — WI-01 recorded 54 cartridges, 66 were
  actually pulled across 4 runs. Deployed code had no decrement to stop it.
- HIGH-1: 133 + 88 cartridges with backing.lotId pointing at LotRecord._id
  instead of the bucket barcode (legacy format).
- HIGH-2: duplicate LotRecord on bucket 5b867012 (qty=1 vs qty=2, 2 days apart).
- MEDIUM: 101 nanoid `_id` CartridgeRecord rows remaining (seed / test fixtures).
- MEDIUM: 242 dangling `inventory_transactions.cartridgeRecordId` references.
- MEDIUM: `manufacturing/+page.server.ts` still using dead `currentPhase` field.
- R1: loadDeck `$inc` atomic but "≤0 → consumed" flip separate → transiently
  negative cartridgeCount possible; no pre-check gate against over-pull.
- R2: `system-backfill` operator stamp on 9,641 waxQc rows — compliance smell.
- R3: cancelRun/abortRun still set `status='backing'` on cartridges (invalid
  post-refactor).
- R5: lineage fields written via `$setOnInsert` → re-scan never updates them.

  rollback on bulkWrite failure

## Session 2026-05-04/05 — Print Barcodes Overhaul + Fridge Breakdown

### Print barcodes (`/manufacturing/print-barcodes`)

Long thread of incremental fixes against a single page. Final state:

- **Print rendering:** entire 8×10 sheet composes onto a single off-DOM
  canvas at 300 DPI → one `<img>` per sheet → one PDF Image object per
  page. Earlier per-cell strategies (canvas, SVG, img+data-URI) all
  tripped HP "PDL PDF limitcheck" because HP firmware caps near 50
  image XObjects/page; one-image-per-sheet is comfortably under that.
- **Layout:** Avery 94102 (8×10 / 0.75" cells / 0.125" gutters /
  0.23"+0.46" page margins) with operator-tuned grid shift (`shiftX
  = 0.1125 * cellSize`, `shiftY = 0.10 * cellSize`) and 15% in-place
  shrink on QR + ABC labels + UUID text (centers preserved). UUID
  text rendered as two lines split at the midpoint.
- **Print stream chrome:** `display:none` on `header`, `aside.mfg-sidebar`,
  `.tron-scanlines` so they never enter the print PDF (was a big
  contributor to the limitcheck before this fix).
- **Print dialog flow:** "Print Sheet" disables itself while open;
  `afterprint` event opens an "Add to inventory?" modal. Trailing
  blank page eliminated by gating `break-after: page` to all-but-last
  sheet.
- **Inventory wiring (final design — commit `8dd3a04`):** consolidated
  to `PT-CT-106` "Barcodes" only (drops the short-lived `PT-CT-115`
  "Barcode Template Sheets" idea). On confirm, two transactions hit
  the same part via the shared `recordTransaction` helper:
    - `consumption: sheetsToPrint`  (sheets fed in)
    - `creation: totalLabels`       (labels printed)
  Net per sheet = +(LABELS_PER_SHEET − 1) = +79 ⇒ WI-01 cartridge-back's
  `if (have < quantity)` check is never starved. `BarcodeInventory`
  legacy mirror still decremented for the cart-mfg-dev "Barcode sheets
  low" alert (one-way drain — pre-existing dysfunction, flagged for
  follow-up).
- **Generate vs Confirm:** `?/print` only mints UUIDs and previews —
  no DB writes. `?/addToInventory` is the commit point; "No, discard"
  just navigates back, no cleanup needed (UUIDs are stateless).

Commits on `dev`: `8bd4e82`, `ef7ee53`, `581dad1`, `bf423f5`, `eab4e18`,
`fe579da`, `01ae0ca`, `8dd3a04`.

## Session 2026-05-04/05 — Laser-cut traceability, pre-wax checkout, MOCREO heartbeat, ask-bims yield fix

Four shipped features, all on `dev`. Each commit referenced inline.

### Laser-cut produced batches mirror as ReceivingLots on PT-CT-112 (`5a5a7be`)

The wax-filling → cartridge-back → laser-cut chain was updating
`PT-CT-112.inventoryCount` via `recordTransaction` (creation tx with
`partDefinitionId`) but not creating a `ReceivingLot`, so internally
produced batches never appeared on the Receiving Lots tab of the part
page (only ROG-accessioned lots did). Fix: in
`src/routes/manufacturing/laser-cutting/+page.server.ts` `recordBatch`,
after the output `recordTransaction`, look up the part doc and create a
matching `ReceivingLot` keyed by `outputLotId` with
`status='accepted'`, `inspectionPathway='coc'`,
`cocMeetsStandards=true`, quantity=stripsProduced.

Backfill: `scripts/backfill-laser-cut-receiving-lots.ts` walks
`laser_cut_batches`, sources qty from the matching InventoryTransaction
(strips/sheet ratio drifted 6→16 between Apr 22 and Apr 27 — recomputing
with current ratio overstates older batches), and skips batches with no
matching tx (predate the partDefinitionId wiring in commit `6795b23`,
never moved inventory). Ran live: 4 lots backfilled (LOT-20260422-RFIZ
qty=18, LOT-20260427-DZCO qty=160, LOT-20260427-OFBC qty=96,
LOT-20260504-MKBM qty=240), 3 pre-wiring orphans skipped. PT-CT-112's
Receiving Lots tab now shows 6 lots / 536 units.

### Pre-wax cartridge removal from a backing lot (`00034b5`)

Operators need to pull cartridges from a backing-lot bucket sitting in
the oven before wax filling (over-counted bucket, inspection pull, etc).
Adds `removeFromBackingLot` action and a second card on
`/manufacturing/scrap`. Operator scans the bucket barcode (which IS the
`BackingLot._id`) and enters a headcount.

Mirrors `wax-filling/loadDeck`: atomic conditional decrement on
`BackingLot.cartridgeCount` via
`findOneAndUpdate({_id, cartridgeCount: {$gte: count}}, {$inc: {-count}})`
which refuses to over-pull, and flips status to `'consumed'` when the
bucket drains. **No CartridgeRecord rows are created or touched** — at
the backing stage cartridges only exist as an aggregate count, individual
records are first created at wax-deck loading. The audit trail lives on
`ManualCartridgeRemoval` (extended with `backingLotId` + `cartridgeCount`,
backward-compat) and an `AuditLog` `CHECKOUT` entry against
`tableName='backing_lots'`.

UI: Recent Checkouts table now badges pre-wax entries with a "Pre-wax"
chip + the lot id; the "Cartridges" expansion is hidden on those rows
(no UUIDs to show). Verified end-to-end via
`scripts/sim-pre-wax-removal.ts` (10 cases, including over-pull
rejection, drain → consumed transition, and post-drain guard).

### MOCREO half-hour heartbeat with reminder cadence + auto-resolve (`cf621bd`)

The 5-min sync at `/api/cron/mocreo` already detected gateway outages
and fired a single email, but never re-emailed and never auto-resolved
— operators stayed in the dark while the gateway stayed down. Adds
`/api/cron/mocreo-heartbeat` at `*/30 * * * *` that re-emails every 30
min while the outage persists and clears the alert + sends a recovery
email when probes start reporting again.

Lifecycle of a `gateway_outage` alert:
- t=0      5-min sync detects outage → creates alert + initial email
- t=30/60  heartbeat sees still-open alert + lastNotifiedAt ≥ 25min ago
           → sends reminder #1, #2, ... (subject prefixes "[REMINDER #N]")
- t=Nm     readings resume → either cron auto-resolves + recovery email

Heartbeat is **DB-only** — it reads `TemperatureReading` and the open
alert from Mongo, never calls the MOCREO API. So it correctly fires on
cascade loss, building power loss, gateway crash, AND if the 5-min sync
itself stops firing for any reason. Reminder threshold is 25min, not 30,
to give the cron 5min of slack so a slightly-late fire doesn't skip a
beat (Vercel cron timing isn't second-precise).

Two guard rails on the email side:
- Defensive create only fires on confirmed outage signal
  (`haveSignal && isOutage`) — no false alarms when there's no probe data
- Auto-resolve requires positive evidence (`haveSignal && !isOutage`).
  An outage that outlasts the 7-day reading lookback returns
  `reminder_sent` (alert stays open, reminders continue) rather than
  falsely emailing "Gateway recovered."

Schema additions on `TemperatureAlert`: `lastNotifiedAt`,
`notificationCount`, `resolvedAt`, `resolvedReason` (all backward-compat).
`notifyGatewayOutage` accepts optional `firstSeenAt` + `reminderNumber`
so reminders render with downtime + reminder count in the subject. New
`notifyGatewayRecovered` for the recovery one-shot. The 5-min sync also
auto-resolves on recovery so both crons converge on the same state
whichever fires first. Verified via `scripts/sim-mocreo-heartbeat.ts`
(10 cases).

### ask-bims `get_run_yield` casing bug (`62ed216`)

User reported "15 wax_stored cartridges still pending QC" but the DB
showed 0. Trace: an AI agent calling `get_run_yield` for run
`7ahsRsv6Y7Hy_1cf1_EPm` (17 carts) reported 0 accepted / 0 scrapped /
17 pendingQc. Real ground truth from `find_cartridges` was 15 Accepted
/ 2 Rejected / 0 pending.

Root cause was **purely casing**, not field path: schema enum is
`['Accepted', 'Rejected', 'Pending']` but the comparisons in
`get_run_yield` were lowercase (`if (qc === 'accepted')`), so no cart
ever matched the accepted or rejected branch and everything fell through
to `pendingQc`. Fixed by normalizing input to lowercase before comparing
(rather than matching capitalized literals) so the bug can't recur if
the schema enum casing ever drifts again.

Subtle gotcha that made this look like a field-path bug: sibling tool
`find_cartridges` projects the same DB field (`waxQc.status`) under the
output name `qcStatus`. The user's diagnostic showed `qcStatus` as a
"top-level field," but it's just an API rename in the projection. Don't
change the field path — `c.waxQc?.status` is the actual DB nesting and
it was already correct in `get_run_yield`.

### Repo layout heads-up

There are TWO git checkouts of this repo on disk:
- Outer: `/Users/brevitest/Bioscale_Operations_System_V2/` (this is the
  one to edit; current branch tracks `origin/dev`)
- Nested: `/Users/brevitest/Bioscale_Operations_System_V2/Bioscale_Operations_System_V2/`
  (separate clone, was on `master`; production deploys from this branch
  via Vercel based on the strips/sheet text observed in production
  inventory transactions)

Caused some confusion mid-session: a `tsx` run from the wrong CWD
resolved scripts under the nested clone. To ship a fix to production,
merge `dev` → `master` so Vercel rebuilds. Untracked `Bioscale_Operations_System_V2/`
in `git status` is the nested clone, not files to commit.

## Session 2026-05-06 — Cart-MFG Checkout: Status-Agnostic Removal + Count Consistency

### Problem

Operator reported: cart-MFG manual checkout page rejected 5 cartridges
with "status is 'completed', expected 'wax_stored'" plus 1 "not found".
The 5 had moved past wax_stored into completed (already QC'd) — system
recorded a state the cart never physically reached. Operator needed a
way to physically remove them from inventory regardless of current
status, without overwriting any production state.

### Resolution

`src/routes/manufacturing/scrap/+page.server.ts` `removeWaxStoredCartridges`
action: dropped the wax_stored eligibility gate. Validate existence
only — any status (wax_stored, scrapped, completed, etc.) is now
checkout-eligible. Status is preserved on checkout. The action records
the physical-removal event in `ManualCartridgeRemoval`, plus a
per-cartridge `AuditLog` entry that captures `statusAtCheckout` so the
audit trail self-describes. Form action name kept (UI compat).

### Count consistency sweep

`getCheckedOutCartridgeIds()` was already in place at
`src/lib/server/checkout-utils.ts` and applied to ~10 location-based
counts (fridge/oven occupancy). It was MISSING from every status-based
count. Patched all of them so a manual checkout drops the cart from
EVERY count simultaneously:
- `routes/manufacturing/+page.server.ts` — phaseCounts, rejectedToday/Week, producedToday/Week
- `routes/cartridge-dashboard/+page.server.ts` — phase/QC/inspection/7-day/assay
- `routes/manufacturing/consumables/+page.server.ts` — phaseCounts + voided/scrapped tile
- `lib/server/ask-bims.ts` — count_cartridges_by_status, get_run_yield
- `lib/server/analytics/runs-feed.ts` — per-run QC aggregation
- `routes/+page.server.ts` — already done in earlier f4062f5 commit

### Skipped on purpose

`lib/server/services/equipment-status.ts:hasReferences` is the
delete-orphan guard. A checked-out cartridge still has a
`waxStorage.locationId` field referencing the fridge — that reference
must still block fridge deletion (otherwise checkouts would let you
delete equipment that has historical refs). Live equipment occupancy
queries already filter (in `/equipment/activity` etc.).

Pre-wax (BackingLot) checkout was already correct via atomic decrement
in `removeFromBackingLot` action.

### Pattern: any new cartridge count query MUST filter

Going forward, every aggregation/count over `CartridgeRecord` that
represents "currently in the system" must include `_id: { $nin:
checkedOutIds }` where `checkedOutIds = await getCheckedOutCartridgeIds()`.
Otherwise checked-out carts double-count. The exception is queries
that intentionally include historical references (delete-orphan
guards, audit reports).

### Heads-up for next session

- The user authorized a sweep to fix all 271 svelte-check errors, but
  paused that work to commit the checkout fix first. A comprehensive
  remediation plan was generated (8 phases, see chat history) — Phase
  1 is `npm install` (4 declared-but-uninstalled packages: resend,
  simple-statistics, @anthropic-ai/sdk, bwip-js).
- Mongoose 9.2.3 is installed but `CLAUDE.md` says Mongoose 8 — three
  errors in `user.ts:108-114` are from Mongoose 9's new
  deleteOne/deleteMany overloads. Worth confirming the upgrade was
  intentional before remediating those.
- `vercel.json` has an uncommitted addition for
  `/api/cron/cartridge-cleanup-reminder` paired with an untracked
  route folder `src/routes/api/cron/cartridge-cleanup-reminder/` — not
  from this session, looks like in-progress work the user has on a
  separate workstream.

## Session 2026-05-06 — 500 fixes, manufacturing pipeline page, status-set centralisation, daily reminder cron

Six shipped pieces. Each commit on `dev`.

### AuditLog enum drop (`b6932c9`, `84d3ef2`)

User reported a 500 on Confirm QC for the post-wax cartridges path,
then a follow-up 500 on the Go Back button on the post-OT-2 assign-
fridge page. Root cause was the AuditLog `action` field's enum:
`['INSERT','UPDATE','DELETE','PHASE_ADVANCE','CHECKOUT']` rejected
the 9+ other action strings the codebase actually writes
(`IMPROPER_ORDER_BLOCKED`, `GO_BACK`, `REASSIGN_STORAGE`, `SYNC`,
`IMPORT`, `UPSERT`, `RETIRE`, `OVERRIDE`, `manual_inventory_edit`,
`create`).

`protectLockedCarts` writes `IMPROPER_ORDER_BLOCKED` whenever a
wax-flow action targets a cart at status ∈ LOCKED_STATUSES. Run
MVJ-YpH5_W-X8WzSmkWY6 had one cart already at status='scrapped'
from a prior `rejectCartridge` per-cart rejection, so every
`completeQC` and `goBack` against it tripped enum validation
mid-flight. The wrapping action 500'd, leaving the run advanced
to Storage but cartridges still at status='wax_filling' with no
`waxFilling.recordedAt`.

First fix added `IMPROPER_ORDER_BLOCKED` to the enum. Then audit
revealed `goBack` writes `action: 'GO_BACK'` a few lines later,
also outside the enum, so the same lot still 500'd. Whitelisting
one value at a time was a losing game (10+ values in use, and the
enum hadn't been load-bearing — the codebase had been silently
hitting these for who knows how long), so the second fix dropped
the enum entirely and made `action` a free-form String. Audit log
is telemetry, not a state machine.

After deploy the user recovered MVJ via standard Confirm QC retry —
no GO_BACK rows ever landed, but the path is open.

Live verification end-of-session: 5 `IMPROPER_ORDER_BLOCKED` rows
have written cleanly post-deploy, 0 enum errors observed since.

### Manufacturing Pipeline page (`002dbf1`)

User asked for "all backing lots in a clear place" then expanded to
"same layout for wax fill, cooling, reagent, seal, store with a
dropdown." Built `/manufacturing/pipeline?stage=<key>` with six
stages and a unified PipelineRow shape (`id/status/count/location/
operator/when/elapsedMin/extras/detailHref`) so one table renders
any stage. Dropdown + chip row both navigate via URL.

Per-stage data sources:
- backing → BackingLot, every status
- wax_fill → WaxFillingRun (active only)
- cooling → CartridgeRecord status='wax_stored' grouped by waxStorage.location
- reagent → ReagentBatchRecord (active only)
- seal → CartridgeRecord status∈{sealed,cured} grouped by topSeal.batchId
- store → CartridgeRecord status='stored' grouped by storage.fridgeName

Wired the cart-mfg-dev "Pipeline Flow" tiles to deep-link into the
matching stage. QC tile stays inert (it's a sub-state of the active
wax run, no canonical entity). Tiles wrap in `<a>` with hover
state; QC stays a `<div>`.

Tailwind dynamic classes (`bg-{stage.color}/20`, `text-{...}`,
`border-{...}/40`) might not be JIT'd because of the opacity
modifiers — flagged for visual verification, not blocking.

Per-row drill-in:
- wax / reagent runs → `/cartridge-admin?runId=...`
- cooling / store fridges → `/equipment/location/[id]`
- backing rows → no canonical drilldown (no per-cartridge view at
  backing stage; cartridges aren't individuated until wax-deck load)

### Run-statuses centralisation (`f4062f5` + `21f9911`)

`WaxFillingRun.status` and `ReagentBatchRecord.status` are loose
`String` with no enum, and the codebase has historically written
values in BOTH cases. Wax leans lowercase ('completed', 'aborted'),
reagent leans capitalized ('Completed', 'Cancelled', 'Aborted'). At
audit time:

  wax_filling_runs:        completed=40 aborted=29 Aborted=3 voided=1
  reagent_batch_records:   Completed=27 Cancelled=13 Aborted=9 completed=1

A `status: { $in: ['completed'] }` filter was finding ~93% of
completed wax runs but only ~4% of completed reagent runs — silent,
persistent miscount across multiple dashboards.

Created `src/lib/server/manufacturing/run-statuses.ts` with named
sets that include both casings: `WAX_PAGE_OWNED`,
`REAGENT_PAGE_OWNED`, `WAX_FILLING_ACTIVE`, `REAGENT_FILLING_ACTIVE`,
`WAX_NON_TERMINAL`, `REAGENT_NON_TERMINAL`, `COMPLETED`,
`ABORTED_OR_CANCELLED`, `VOIDED`, `ANY_TERMINAL`.

First sweep fixed:
- `manufacturing/resource-locks.ts` (already had constants — switch
  to module imports)
- `routes/+page.server.ts` (home dashboard active-wax filter)
- `routes/equipment/activity/+page.server.ts` (equipment dashboard,
  was missing reagent runs)
- `routes/api/cron/daily-digest/+server.ts` (already both-case inline
  — switched to module)
- `routes/manufacturing/pipeline/+page.server.ts` (built earlier same
  session — switched to ANY_TERMINAL)

Audit caught three I missed and labelled "comprehensive" too early:
- `manufacturing/wax-filling/equipment/+page.server.ts` (FOUR filters
  — the worst was reagent history hiding 96% of records)
- `manufacturing/wax-filling/oven-queue/+page.server.ts`
- `manufacturing/reagent-filling/+page.server.ts:203` (cosmetic
  duplicate of WAX_PAGE_OWNED)

End-of-session verification: a `$in: ['completed','Completed']`
query against reagent_batch_records returns 28 rows; the lowercase-
only version returns 1. Centralisation recovered 27 runs that
dashboards were silently hiding.

### Pre-wax checkout regression sim (`a3a21df`)

Companion script to `sim-mocreo-heartbeat.ts`. Replicates the
`removeFromBackingLot` action's logic against a synthetic test
BackingLot: happy path / over-pull / drain → consumed / post-drain
guard / nonexistent-lot guard / validation. 10 cases. Was used to

### Daily 4:30 PM cartridge-cleanup reminder cron (`d4d8dcc`)

User wanted a daily email reminder to ncox@brevitest.com via the
internal email system (not Slack/MCP). Built
`/api/cron/cartridge-cleanup-reminder` and registered it in
vercel.json at `30 21 * * *` (21:30 UTC = 4:30 PM CDT). Hard-coded
recipient by request. Email body links to Equipment Activity,
Pipeline → Backing, and Cartridge Checkout — the three surfaces
the operator needs to reconcile drift.

Earlier same session I'd created a one-shot claude.ai routine for
4:30 PM today as a stop-gap; disabled it once the daily cron
landed so today doesn't get a duplicate at 4:30.

Note: Vercel only fires crons from production deployments. The
new entry is registered on the dev preview but won't actually
fire until dev → master is merged. User said they'd handle the
merge themselves.

### Backing-timer enforcement check (no code change)

User created backing lot `d0c6ce18-cfac-46b4-8e42-1bd8370ee169`
(70 carts, Oven 1, by nick) and asked whether the heating-time
restriction is being enforced. Replicated the
`scanBackingLot` server-side conditional from `wax-filling/+page.
server.ts:411` against the live lot — confirmed:

- `scanBackingLot` returns HTTP 400 if `elapsedMin < minOvenTimeMin`
  with message "Lot not ready. {N} minute(s) remaining (minimum
  {threshold} min required). Wait, pick a bucket that has passed
  the timer, or get an admin to override."
- `loadDeck` does NOT independently re-check the timer; it pulls
  `activeLotId` from `WaxFillingRun.activeLotId`, which is only
  set when `scanBackingLot` succeeds. The UI gates cartridge-scan
  inputs behind the lot scan, so a normal operator can't bypass.
  A direct POST to `?/loadDeck` could skip the timer + write
  cartridges with `backing.lotId=null`. Worth knowing; not in the
  attack surface for the wax-filling page.

User noted the threshold is set to 30 in DB (default fallback in
code is 60); they said they'd flip it to 60 themselves on the
wax-filling settings page.

## Session 2026-05-06 (cont.) — Audit-log dual-collection consolidation

While verifying the cart-MFG checkout went through cleanly, found Mongo
had TWO audit collections side-by-side:
- `audit_log` (singular, 10,643 entries) — wired to the AuditLog
  Mongoose model at `src/lib/server/db/models/audit-log.ts:28`. All
  production app code writes here.
- `audit_logs` (plural, 269 entries) — created entirely by hand-run
  scripts in `/scripts` that called `db.collection('audit_logs')`
  directly, bypassing the model. ~95% of plural entries are from
  `system-*` script-author tags.

Production was always correct. The plural was pure script drift.

Resolution:
1. `scripts/consolidate-audit-logs.ts` (new) — migration tool with
   `--dry-run` and `--execute` flags. Dry-run confirmed 0 `_id`
   collisions; `--execute` copied all 269 plural entries into singular
   (singular grew 10644 → 10913) and dropped the plural collection.
2. Sed-replaced `audit_logs` → `audit_log` across 53 script files and
   3 src/ comment references. Without this step, the next run of any
   diagnostic script would silently re-create the plural collection.
3. Two new diagnostic scripts saved:
   `scripts/verify-recent-checkout.ts` and
   `scripts/verify-checkout-audit-log.ts` — useful for future post-
   checkout sanity passes.

Commit: `c1ba3fa chore(audit-log): consolidate dual audit_log/audit_logs
collections into one`. 58 files changed (53 script renames + 3 src/
comment edits + migration tool + 2 diag scripts).

Going forward: AuditLog collection name is `audit_log` (singular). The
Mongoose model is the only sanctioned writer. If a one-off script must
use the raw driver, it MUST use `'audit_log'` not `'audit_logs'`.

## Session 2026-05-06 (cont. 2) — 271-error remediation, all 8 phases shipped

User authorised editing `.svelte` files for this remediation despite
the CLAUDE.md "UI is frozen" rule. End state: `npm run check` reports
0 errors (was 271). Single commit `6dcd6a7`, 69 files changed, +432 /
-282 lines. Mongoose upgrade to 9.2.3 confirmed intentional;
CLAUDE.md updated to match.

### Phase recap

1. **`npm install`** — 4 declared-but-uninstalled packages were
   missing from `node_modules`, cascading errors across importing
   files. Reinstall cleared 69 errors in one shot.

2. **Dead-reference fixups** — `r2Upload` in `_receiving/new` was a
   misnamed `uploadToR2` with arg-order swap. `parts/[partId]/+page.svelte`
   referenced `IpRevisionHistory` + `IpFormDefinitionEditor` +
   `currentRevision` without imports/derivations.

3. **Schema drift in load functions (the big category, 115+ errors)**
   — 15 hot-spot routes had load functions hand-crafting narrow
   projections that dropped fields the templates still read. Fields
   all existed on the underlying Mongoose models — server-side
   projections were the only thing missing. Touched: cartridge-admin/
   release, /failures, /filled, /statistics, /sku-management;
   customers/[id]; assembly/[sessionId]; assays/[assayId]/assign;
   assays/import; cartridges/analysis;
   documents/instructions/[id]/run/[runId]; cartridges/[cartridgeId];
   parts/+page; equipment/robots; equipment/activity.

4. **Type alignment** — Dropped `LifecycleStage` casing constraint
   in cartridge-admin/failures + /filled (UI uses post-state
   PascalCase, not pipeline lowercase). Added `'assay_loaded'` and
   `'testing'` to `LifecycleStage`. Mongoose 9 hook signature
   regression in `user.ts` (`.pre('deleteOne'/'deleteMany'/
   'findOneAndDelete')` now mistyped) — cast through `any` since
   runtime hooks still fire correctly. Fixed `r2.ts` `fetch(buffer)`
   by wrapping in `Uint8Array` (newer Node `fetch` doesn't accept
   Node `Buffer` directly). Added `'cut_top_seal'` to
   `inventory-transaction` enum. Widened `TronButton` variant union
   to include `'ghost'`.

5. **Native-driver casts (Mongoose ObjectId vs nanoid string)** —
   New helpers `byId(id)` / `asId(id)` in
   `src/lib/server/db/native-helpers.ts`. Replaced 13 sites across
   6 files where raw `db.collection(...)` calls hit the
   `Type 'string' is not assignable to type 'Condition<ObjectId>'`
   problem. Single source of cast.

6. **Possibly-undefined guards** — `locals.user!` non-null
   assertions in actions where `requirePermission` already narrows
   but TypeScript doesn't propagate (manufacturing/wax-filling,
   /reagent-filling, /opentron-control/reagent, equipment/decks-trays,
   qa-qc). Optional-chaining for scattered `form.failures` /
   `data.spu` reads.

7. **Implicit-any annotations** — `(x: any)` on ~30 callback
   parameters across ~10 .svelte files. Pure type hygiene.

8. **Last-mile** — Reordered `$derived` `tubesUsed` below the
   `$state` variables it referenced in wax-creation. Expanded
   wax-filling `runState` type to include `activeLotId`. Expanded
   wax/reagent `RobotRunState` with `activeProcess?` field. Added
   missing `WaxCartridgeRecord` export to `schema.ts`. The
   stubborn `equipment/activity` errors turned out to be the
   .svelte's OWN `Props` interface declaring a narrow `locations`
   shape — server-side fixes never propagated because the .svelte
   was overriding. Widened the Props interface to match.

### Important pattern: .svelte Props interfaces win over PageData

`equipment/activity/+page.svelte` and several other components
declare their own `interface Props { data: { ... } }` block that
shadows the auto-generated PageData type. If you widen a load
function and the .svelte STILL shows "property X doesn't exist",
check whether the .svelte is type-asserting its data shape locally.

### byId() / asId() helper

`src/lib/server/db/native-helpers.ts` is the new home for raw
MongoDB driver casts. Mongoose models are typed correctly for
nanoid `_id`s, but native-driver calls
(`db.collection('foo').findOne({ _id: stringId })`) hit
`Type 'string' is not assignable to type 'Condition<ObjectId>'`.
Use `byId(id)` for filters, `asId(id)` for `_id` fields in
`insertOne` payloads.

- Build + contract test gates not yet run at time of writing this
  note — needed before the dev → master merge.
- Phase 3 surfaced real data into templates that previously got
  `undefined`. Hidden bugs in date formatters / number math could
  trip on those routes. Worth a 5-minute smoke test of each fixed
  page.
- The nested checkout `Bioscale_Operations_System_V2/Bioscale_Operations_System_V2/`
  is what Vercel ships from. Today's changes are only on the outer
  copy and only on `dev`. Production needs a `dev → master` merge
  to pick them up.

  remains uncommitted (separate workstream — left untouched).

## Session 2026-05-11 — Scanner phase-0 end-to-end on B07 + fleet rollout

End-to-end success: gantry-mounted Waveshare scanner on OT-2 B07 now
teaches positions, sweeps them, and feeds barcodes into wax/reagent
filling. Cloned the position set to R04 and B14 as a starting point.

### Bridge & hardware (B07)
- Bridge daemon (`scanner-bridge.py`) running on B07 as PID 32558 against
  `https://bioscale-operations-system-mongodb.vercel.app`. Heartbeats
  every 10s land in `scanner_events` (deviceId=`ot2-b07-scanner`).
- USB cable reseating mid-session shifted the device between `/dev/ttyACM0`
  and `/dev/ttyACM1`. Solved permanently with a udev rule installed at
  `/etc/udev/rules.d/99-bims-scanner.rules` (VID:PID `0218:0212`,
  serial `2027300413413333`) → pins to `/dev/scanner`. `.env` now reads
  `SCANNER_SERIAL_PORT=/dev/scanner`. Root remounted ro afterwards.

### Teach UI fixes (commits on `feat/scanner-phase-0`)
- `3c21e98 fix(scanner-teach): surface real fetch errors instead of bare "fetch failed"`
  Proxy wraps fetch to expose `err.cause.code` + URL. Server logs the
  underlying reason via console.warn. Client api() augments network errors
  with the URL.
- `19ef4ca fix(scanner-teach): two bugs blocking Open Maintenance Run`
  (1) OT-2 `/maintenance_runs` requires `{ data: {} }` envelope, not `{}`.
  (2) `loadPipette` rejected display-name strings like "20 microliter" —
  resolve the canonical name from live `/pipettes` instead of trusting
  the set's stored `pipetteName`; respect the set's `pipetteMount`.
- `21bcef5 fix(scanner-teach): derive deviceId from robot name`
  Was hardcoded `lab-mac-scanner-1`. Now derived via regex on robot.name
  (`Robot 3 B07` → `ot2-b07-scanner`).

### Settings list UX
- `c69ec41 feat(scanner-teach): per-card delete on the settings list`
  Each set card gets an inline `×` button posting to the per-set
  deleteSet action, with a confirmation prompt and an inline failure
  banner. Avoids navigating into each set to clean up.

### Position extrapolation
- `scripts/extrapolate-scanner-positions.ts` — pops slots 8-23 from
  slots 0-7 using `x + 148 mm * column`, preserving Y and Z. Used on
  B07's "Barcode Locations" set after the operator taught the first 8.
- Snake-numbered deck: col 0 = slots 1-8 top→bot; col 1 = 9-16 bot→top;
  col 2 = 17-24 top→bot. Same-row slots are colinear in Y. Source
  column was alternating-X (~3 vs ~75) intentionally; extrapolation
  preserves the alternation.

### Filling pages — auto-scan + per-slot rescan + override
- `ab7e620 feat(scanner): note localhost-only auto-scan on wax + reagent grids`
  Amber notice under the Scan Cartridges card. Vercel can't route to

  a Mac on the lab network.
- `bc26bb6 feat(scanner-sweep): maxSlots cap + retry-once + correct deviceId default`
  `/api/scanner/sweep` accepts body.maxSlots; per-slot trigger retries
  once before recording failure; default deviceId derived from robot
  name. Audit + response include slotsWalked.
- `49b22cc feat(wax-filling): per-slot rescans, override, sweep count + retry-aware grid`
  Wax DeckLoadingGrid: scans state now sparse Array<scan|null> length 24
  (slotIndex matches deck position). Count input next to Scan Cartridges
  button (default = plannedCartridgeCount). Sweep no longer aborts on
  per-slot failures — collects them into sweepFailures, surfaces summary,

  Same refactor on reagent grid.

  Audit-found bugs: override was bypassing cross-slot duplicate check
  (user-intent was only "replace what's in this slot"); manual rescan
  cleared red border but not the failures list. Fixed both grids.

- `scripts/extrapolate-scanner-positions.ts` — see above.
- `scripts/clone-scanner-position-set.ts` — see above.

## Session 2026-05-11 (evening) / 2026-05-12 — Scanner phase-1: async sweep, live UI, real cancel, perf tuning

### Headline
Scanner sweep is now production-feeling: live progress in the UI, working
pause/cancel/resume, history page, and ~2-3s/slot instead of 6s. The
session also surfaced two real bugs that had been silently masking
themselves for the entire prior session.

### Big architectural change — async sweep with persisted state
- New `OpentronsScannerSweepRun` model — live state + history for every
  Scan Cartridges invocation. Status (running/paused/cancelled/completed/
  errored), slotsDone, currentSlotIndex, scans[], errors[], appended log[]
  with timestamps and per-level color coding.
- `POST /api/scanner/sweep` is now fire-and-forget: validates inputs,
  rejects if a sweep is running on the same robot, creates the SweepRun
  doc, fires the work in the background, returns the sweepRunId
  immediately. Response no longer hangs for ~2 minutes.
- Background worker writes progress at every step: maintenance-run-open,
  pipette discovery, home, per-slot move + scan attempts, retries, errors,
  home, run close. Between every slot it re-reads cancelRequested /
  pauseRequested and honors them.
- New endpoints:
  - `GET /api/scanner/sweep/<id>` — snapshot for polling
  - `POST /api/scanner/sweep/<id>` with action='cancel'|'pause'|'resume'
  - `GET /api/scanner/sweep` — paginated history list
- Commits: `16032c6` server, `23b8970` client live UI on both filling
  grids, `7e9ba2d` history page at /manufacturing/opentron-control/sweeps,
  `56ded5e` override-semantics audit fix.

### Two silent bugs found that had been masking themselves
1. `sendMaintenanceCommand` in maintenance.ts only checked res.ok (HTTP
   201). OT-2 returns 201 even when the inner command failed —
   body.data.status="failed" + body.data.error.detail. Failed moves were
   passing as success, gantry never moved, scanner re-read the same
   barcode N times, sweep reported `scanCount: 24, errorCount: 0`. Fixed
   in `b363609`: detect inner status, throw with the OT-2's errorType +
   detail.
2. Stored positions had z=200 across all three robots' sets. p300_single
   _gen2 on the left mount rejects z>~170 with FailedToPlanMoveError.
   Combined with bug 1, this was the actual root cause of "gantry homes
   then scans without moving". Mitigated by global -50mm Z shift via the
   new `scripts/shift-scanner-positions.ts` (commit `666bdfd`). Real
   solution is re-teach with a workable Z.

### Cancel works even when the worker is wedged
- Pre-fix: cancel was just a DB flag flip. If the worker was hung inside
  a Node fetch (e.g., on `openMaintenanceRun` after a dev-server restart
  left stuck keepalive sockets), nothing read the flag and the gantry
  stayed put forever.
- Fix in `e4e0ce4`:
  - Cancel handler now immediately sets `status='cancelled'` on the doc
    so the UI flips out of "running" instantly.
  - Then actively queries the OT-2 for `/maintenance_runs/current_run`
    and calls closeMaintenanceRun on whatever id comes back. Releases
    motor holds AND makes the wedged worker's next OT-2 call fail with
    "run not found" so it exits cleanly.
  - `robotFetch` now wraps every OT-2 fetch in a 30s AbortSignal — stuck
    keepalive sockets abort instead of hanging forever. 30s matches the
    OT-2's own waitUntilComplete window, so legitimate slow moves still
    complete.

### Perf tuning — ~6s/slot → ~2-3s/slot
Per-slot timing measured at the start of the session was 6032ms avg with
123ms idle gap (worker overhead negligible). Three targeted changes:
- `moveTo` defaults forceDirect=true. Scanner sweeps don't pick up tips
  or transport liquid; the safety-arc routing was pure overhead. -500ms-1s
  per slot. (`17c09b0`)
- Sweep DB-poll dropped 200ms→50ms.
- Bridge .env on B07: POLL_INTERVAL_MS 500→100, SCAN_TIMEOUT_S 3→1.5.
- **Biggest win — bridge swapped to localhost target.** BIMS_BASE_URL
  changed from `https://bioscale-operations-system-mongodb.vercel.app`
  to `http://172.16.28.173:5176` on B07 .env. Every trigger-poll and
  event-POST hop drops from ~150-250ms to ~5-10ms. Requires
  `AGENT_API_KEY` to be present in the Mac's local `.env` (now is —
  fetched from the bridge .env and persisted). For production sweeps,
  the bridge needs to be repointed back at Vercel.

### Test-mode backing lot (no inventory impact)
Commit `8b28d55`: wax-filling "Test Mode" checkbox actually functions
now. Was previously just a label — sent override=true which only
triggered the admin cure-time path. Now sends testMode=true; server
auto-creates `TEST-LOT-<runId>` with cartridgeCount=24, status=ready, no
admin re-auth required. Real BackingLot inventory untouched.

### Robot name alignment + wax start-screen header fix
- `Equipment.name` updated to match `OpentronsRobot.name` ("Robot 3" →
  "Robot 3 B07" etc.) via `scripts/align-equipment-robot-names.ts` (commit
  `3777bd1`). The link between collections is the shared _id; rename is
  purely cosmetic.
- Wax-filling start-screen "Robot N — Wax Filling" header was hardcoded
  to `data.robotId === 'robot-1' ? 'Robot 1' : 'Robot 2'` — never matched
  the nanoid IDs, always fell through to "Robot 2". Now uses data.robotName
  pulled from the layout's robots list (`9b8252b`).

### Scripts added under /scripts
- `extrapolate-scanner-positions.ts` — fill slots 8-23 from 0-7 using
  x+148mm per column (preserves alternating-X within column).
- `clone-scanner-position-set.ts` — copy a set across robots with optional
  --default + demote existing defaults. Used in this session to seed R04
  and B14 from B07.
- `shift-scanner-positions.ts` — global dx/dy/dz nudge on a set. Used to
  push z=200 → z=150.
- `align-equipment-robot-names.ts` — Equipment.name ← OpentronsRobot.name.
- `inspect-scanner-events.ts` — parameterized event tail by deviceId.
  Replaces the deprecated `inspect-r04-scanner.ts`.

### Pending / next session
- **Re-teach Z properly** on at least B07. The -50mm shift may not put
  the scanner in optimal focal range — test reads first. If empty-payload
  failures show up, re-teach Z per slot.
- **R04 + B14 still untested** — coordinates copied verbatim from B07,

- **Bridge BIMS_BASE_URL** points at the Mac. Revert to Vercel
  (`https://bioscale-operations-system-mongodb.vercel.app`) when no
  longer testing from this Mac, or production sweeps from elsewhere
  will be slow / unreachable.
- **Mongoose warning**: `errors` is a reserved schema pathname on
  OpentronsScannerSweepRun. Functional but worth renaming to `slotErrors`
  for cleanliness.
- **Vite startup time** is ~10s on first request after restart — Node
  module cache warm-up. Worth investigating if it bites again.
- **`npm run dev` doesn't load .env** into process.env (existing memory).
  Start with `set -a; source .env; set +a; npm run dev` or the server
  500s on every request with "MONGODB_URI is not set".

## Session 2026-05-15 — Kanban auto-archive (PRD + implementation)

### Headline
Per-task Archive button on `/kanban/task/[taskId]` was 404-ing (the
`?/archive` form action did not exist server-side). Wired the action,
hardened the existing `/api/cron/archive-done-tasks` endpoint so Vercel
Cron can actually call it, scheduled it weekly (Sunday 23:00 CDT / Monday
04:00 UTC), and added a one-shot cleanup script that ran a full sweep of
existing done tasks regardless of age.

### Decisions (confirmed with Jacob)
- Schedule: `0 4 * * 1` UTC = Sunday 11 PM CDT. Board is clean Monday AM.
- Cron min-age: 24h since `statusChangedAt`. Stops a task marked done at
  10:59 PM Sunday from disappearing 60 seconds later.
- Per-task archive: done-only. Non-done tasks return `fail(400)` and the
  task is unchanged. (UI is frozen, so the button stays visible — server
  is source of truth.)

### Files changed
- `docs/prds/KANBAN-AUTO-ARCHIVE.md` — new PRD
- `src/routes/kanban/task/[taskId]/+page.server.ts` — added `archive` action
  with done-status guard, activityLog push, and AuditLog row
- `src/routes/api/cron/archive-done-tasks/+server.ts` — added GET, 3-mode
  auth (CRON_SECRET / `vercel-cron/*` UA / agent API key), 24h min-age
  filter on `statusChangedAt`
- `vercel.json` — added cron entry at `0 4 * * 1`
- `scripts/archive-done-tasks-cleanup.ts` — one-shot manual archive of
  all `status: 'done', archived: false` tasks (no min-age) with single
  `audit_log` row recording the bulk operation

### Validation
- `npm run check`: 26 pre-existing ERRORS (missing optional deps + unrelated
  implicit-anys) and 416 warnings. Zero new errors in changed files.
- Cleanup script run: archived all currently-done tasks (see audit_log
  row `recordId: 'manual-cleanup'`, `changedBy: 'system-manual'`).

### Branch state
- All on `feature/kanban-improvement`, branched off freshly-pulled `dev`.
- 4 feature files + this progress entry + cleanup script committed and
  pushed. Carryover stray `s` in `docs/BIMS-UNIFIED-TIMELINE-SPEC.md` left
  unstaged (Jacob editing in the IDE).

  `cron-bulk` row and the board is clean.
- Open question: do we want a one-click "unarchive" affordance on
  `/kanban/archived`? Currently archive is one-way through the UI.

### Follow-up — Archived view UX (same session)

After deploying, swapped the Archive table's last column from "Archived"
(archivedAt) to "Completed" (statusChangedAt). Reasoning: once a task is
in the archive, the timestamp Jacob actually wants is "when did we finish
this," not "when did the cron sweep it up." Since archiving doesn't change
`status` (the field flips `archived: true` but leaves `status: 'done'`
intact), `statusChangedAt` on an archived task is necessarily the moment
it moved to done.

Added click-to-sort on all five archive column headers (Title, Project,
Assignee, Status, Completed). Three-state cycle per header: asc → desc →
cleared. Matches the existing pattern on `/kanban/list/+page.svelte`.
Null `statusChangedAt` sorts to the bottom via `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER`.

- `src/routes/kanban/archived/+page.svelte` — sort state, sortedTasks
  derivation, header click handlers, header sort indicators, column rename.
- Commit `b3198c1`. Pushed to `feature/kanban-improvement`.

### Follow-up — Backlog accordion on board view (same session)

Backlog column is now collapsible per project, defaulting to collapsed.
The motivation: backlog is the tallest column and dominates the project
section's vertical height. With backlog folded, the project's row is
sized by the tallest of Ready / WIP / Waiting / Done — much more
information-dense.

`KanbanColumn.svelte` gained three optional props — `collapsible`,
`collapsed`, `onToggleCollapse` — that are backward-compatible (default
false / undefined → behavior identical to before). When `collapsible`,
the header gets a chevron button that rotates 90° when collapsed. The
outer column div still receives drop events when collapsed, so dragging
a task onto a folded backlog header still works.

`/kanban/+page.svelte` tracks fold state per project in a new
`collapsedBacklogs: Set<string | null>` state, initialized from
`data.projects` (= all backlogs collapsed at mount).

- `src/lib/components/kanban/KanbanColumn.svelte` — collapsible props,
  chevron, gated task list render
- `src/routes/kanban/+page.svelte` — collapsedBacklogs state, toggleBacklog,
  prop passthrough on the per-column `<KanbanColumn>` call

### Follow-up — Persistent collapse state on board (same session)

Project section fold + backlog accordion fold are now stored on the
KanbanProject document. Global, not per-user: every kanban:read user
sees the same state, every kanban:write user can change it. The change
survives refresh and is shared across all users on next refresh.

Schema additions on `kanban_projects`:
- `collapsed: Boolean` — defaults `false` (project section starts expanded)
- `backlogCollapsed: Boolean` — defaults `true` (backlog starts collapsed)

Legacy projects without the fields fall through to those defaults via
normalization in the layout load (`p.collapsed === true`,
`p.backlogCollapsed !== false`). The earlier "auto-collapse projects with
no tasks" heuristic was removed per Jacob's decision — explicit state
only.

Persistence API: `POST /api/kanban/projects/[id]/ui-state` with body
`{ collapsed?: boolean, backlogCollapsed?: boolean }`. Partial updates
allowed. Requires `kanban:write`. No audit log entry — UI state is not
data, just a presentation toggle.

Client pattern: optimistic update in the local Set, fire-and-forget POST,
revert on non-ok response. No global error banner since the failure
mode is invisible (user just sees the chevron snap back).

- `src/lib/server/db/models/kanban-project.ts` — two new schema fields
- `src/routes/kanban/+layout.server.ts` — normalize into definite booleans
- `src/routes/api/kanban/projects/[id]/ui-state/+server.ts` — new endpoint
- `src/routes/kanban/+page.svelte` — drop heuristic, init from DB,
  async toggle handlers with optimistic+revert

### Side errand — One-off cartridge status flip (same session)

Mid-session, Jacob asked for a quick programmatic fix unrelated to the
kanban work: flip status from `stored` → `completed` on 16 specific
cartridge records (UUIDs hard-coded into the script).

`scripts/oneoff-cartridge-stored-to-completed.ts` does this with safety
guards: skips records that are missing, not currently `stored`, or have
`finalizedAt` set. Bypasses Mongoose sacred middleware by using raw
`db.collection('cartridge_records').updateOne(...)` — the explicit
finalizedAt check substitutes for that safeguard. One `audit_log` row
per cartridge (`tableName: 'cartridge_records'`, `oldData: {status: 'stored'}`,
`newData: {status: 'completed'}`, `changedBy: 'system-manual'`).

Result: 16 updated, 0 missing, 0 wrong status, 0 finalized.

Script left in the repo as a historical record (matches the existing
`complete-stuck-wax-run-2y2f.ts` / `delete-fridge-001-11-cartridges.ts`
pattern) but currently **unstaged** — Jacob said to handle it separately
from the kanban feature branch.

### Follow-up — Persistence reliability (same session)

Jacob tested the deploy and reported "only some of the project horizontal
swim lanes persisted" after folding several then navigating away.
Diagnosis: fire-and-forget POSTs got orphaned when the page unmounted
mid-flight — fast ones completed, slow ones (Vercel cold-start) got
dropped.

Fix in `persistProjectUiState`:
- `keepalive: true` on the fetch — POST survives page unload.
- Retry once after 500ms on 5xx or network error. 4xx errors aren't
  retried (won't be fixed by retrying auth/validation failures).

Single ~15-line change in `src/routes/kanban/+page.svelte`.

### Follow-up — Stale layout data on within-kanban navigation (same session)

Jacob hit a different bug: fold state didn't persist when navigating
between sibling kanban routes (/kanban → /kanban/list → /kanban). Worked
fine on hard refresh or cross-layout navigation.

Root cause: SvelteKit caches `+layout.server.ts` data and doesn't re-run
the load function on sibling-route navigations. So `data.projects`
stayed stale (pre-click `collapsed` values) even after the DB POST
succeeded. On return to /kanban, the page component re-mounted and
the `$state(...)` Set initializer read the stale cached layout data.

Fix:
- `kanban/+layout.server.ts` — `depends('kanban:projects')` tags the
  layout for explicit invalidation.
- `kanban/+page.svelte` — `invalidate('kanban:projects')` after each
  successful UI-state POST. Layout re-runs in the background, so the
  next time the page remounts (sibling-route navigation), its $state
  initializer reads fresh `data.projects`.

Note: $state initializers only run once at mount — they don't re-run
when `data` changes during the component's lifetime. That's intentional
here: the optimistic local state IS the source of truth while the page
is open. The invalidation ensures the SERVER data catches up so future
remounts start from the correct baseline.

### Follow-up — Nav cleanup on kanban layout (same session)

## Session 2026-06-09 — Scanner debounce + ship CV/capture to production + fix capture 500

### Headline

Got the Pi-station capture workflow working end-to-end in production:
fixed a scanner double-scan bug, deployed the entire CV/capture suite
from `dev` to `main` (production), then chased two runtime 500s on
`/api/cv/capture` until photos save and the 2-photo workflow advances.
Operator confirmed live: scan → lock → Space → photo saves → 1/2 → 2/2.

### What shipped (commit chain)

- 1cd1d05a (bims-capture-agent) — scanner sensing-mode debounce. A
  scanner in sensing mode re-fires the same barcode many times/sec;
  because `cartridgeId` is only set after the async lookup resolves, a
  burst of re-reads each kicked off a duplicate `/api/cv/lookup-cartridge`
  call (double "Locked on X" banner + double session reset). Added an
  in-flight `locking` guard in handleScan (mirrors the `submitting`
  pattern): scan once, lock once. Merged to dev as 417d57fa.
- d1b1721f (main) — merged `dev` into `main` (433 commits). This is what
  put the whole CV/capture station, /cv/projects, /cv/stations,
  /cv/label, etc. into PRODUCTION for the first time. Clean merge, no
  conflicts.
- a0ffad5a + 6276cba4 (main) — the capture 500 fix, two parts (below).
  Cherry-picked to dev as 6913bba4 + 108cd14b.

### The capture 500 — root cause + fix

Two stacked bugs, surfaced one after the other by testing on prod:

1. Legacy data: 475 of ~756 `cartridge_records` have `photos` stored as
   `{}` (object) instead of `[]`. The endpoint's `$push: { photos }`
   throws "must be an array but is of type … in document {_id:<cart>}"
   → 500 on every capture to a bad cart. (This was the long-known,
   previously-deferred photos[] defect — see memory.)
   Fix: replaced `$push` with a pipeline update that coerces photos to
   `[]` when it isn't an array, then appends — self-heals each cart on
   first capture:
     [{ $set: { photos: { $concatArrays: [
        { $cond: [{ $isArray: '$photos' }, '$photos', []] },
        { $literal: [photoEntry] } ] } } }]

2. Mongoose 9 gotcha: array (aggregation-pipeline) updates THROW at
   runtime — "Cannot pass an array to query updates unless the
   `updatePipeline` option is set" — unless you pass
   `{ updatePipeline: true }` as the options arg. This is NOT caught by
   `npm run check` (the types accept the array), so it passed local
   type-check and only failed live. Fixed by adding the option.

### Knowledge learned (reusable — read before similar work)

- VERCEL PREVIEW ENV: Preview deployments only get env vars scoped to
  the Preview environment. Production-only secrets (R2_WORKER_URL,
  R2_UPLOAD_SECRET, R2_PUBLIC_URL, R2_BUCKET_NAME — see
  src/lib/server/services/r2.ts) are ABSENT on branch/preview deploys, so
  `/api/cv/capture` 500s with "R2_WORKER_URL not configured" on a preview
  even though scan/lookup (Mongo reads) work fine. To test capture either
  run on a deploy that has the R2 vars, or add them to the Preview scope.
- PRODUCTION BRANCH = `main`. Repo has THREE plausible prod branches
  (main, master, dev). Confirmed this session: Vercel production deploys
  from `main` (merge → prod URL updated → capture worked). `dev` is the
  active integration branch and was ~400 commits AHEAD of main/master;
  main/master had been stale since Apr/May. master and main have also
  diverged from each other historically.
- MONGOOSE 9 pipeline updates need `{ updatePipeline: true }` (runtime,
  not type-checked). See above.
- LOCAL BUILD ON WINDOWS can't finish `@sveltejs/adapter-vercel`: OOM at
  Node's 4GB default, and even at 8GB it dies on
  `EBUSY readlink 'C:\swapfile.sys'` (the Vercel file-tracer choking on
  the Windows swap file). Both are LOCAL-only; Vercel's Linux builders
  are fine. The meaningful local signal is the Vite build itself
  (`✓ built in …`) — that's what catches real code errors; the adapter
  packaging step can be ignored locally.
- PI STATION VIDEO is a DIRECT browser↔Pi WebRTC connection
  (`wss://<station>/ws`). BIMS is NOT in the media path — it only mints a
  5-min HS256 JWT signed with `station.jwtSecret` from Mongo
  (deployment-independent). So switching BIMS deployments cannot break
  video. "No video" this session was a stale connection from an old tab;
  reconnect/refresh fixed it. The Pi agent supports `trigger_scan` and
  tags scans `triggered` (agent.py); station capture is a browser-side
  canvas snapshot of the WebRTC frame POSTed to /api/cv/capture.

### Branch state

main = production, live + verified. dev = synced (108cd14b).
bims-capture-agent retains the original scanner-debounce commit.

### Open / deferred

- Orphan data: pre-fix 500s left `CvImage` docs + R2 files with no
  `photos[]` back-ref (CvImage.create + R2 upload ran BEFORE the failing
  $push) and bumped `photoSequence`. Cosmetic; a cleanup script can prune
  them if tidy data is wanted.
- Root cause of the `{}` photos fields never investigated; bad data still
  exists at rest (capture only heals carts as they're photographed).
- Any OTHER write path that `$push`es to `photos[]` will hit the same
  non-array 500 — apply the same coercion + updatePipeline pattern.

## Session 2026-06-11 (cont.) — OT-2 command bridge (OT2-BRIDGE PRDs 1-2) + wax math fix

### Architecture decision (Jacob)
Outbound-only command bridge = the standing BIMS IoT control-plane pattern
(robot arm + CV stations migrate later). Two-tier rule: control plane =
device polls BIMS (works from Vercel, zero inbound exposure); heavy realtime
data plane (video) = direct browser<->device on LAN with BIMS-minted tokens.
One unified daemon per OT-2 (replaces scanner-bridge.py). v1 scope: filling
flows + sweep + deck scan + teach/jog + health; opentrons-clone stays
direct-only. PRDs: docs/prds/OT2-BRIDGE-1/-2.

  SCANNER-OT2-DEPLOYMENT.md; stop old scanner-bridge first; B07->R04->B14).

  break shebangs on the robot).

## Session 2026-06-12 — Prod dynamic-API 404 root-caused + fixed (Vercel /api collision)

### The bug
Every dynamic `[param]` API endpoint (a `+server.ts` under a bracket dir) 404'd
on the production Vercel alias, while static API routes AND dynamic PAGE routes
worked. This silently broke the OT-2 bridge: robots execute commands fine, but
the daemon's result/progress callbacks (`/api/agent/ot2/commands/[id]/result`
+ `/progress`) 404'd, so BIMS never recorded outcomes — and the wax one-button
Start Run (deck-scan / sweep `[id]` round-trips) could not complete.

### Investigation (what we ruled OUT, with evidence)
Probed prod with curl + header inspection. The discriminator: `x-vercel-id`.
Broken routes returned `x-vercel-error: NOT_FOUND` with `cle1::` ONLY (rejected
at the edge, never routed to a function); working routes showed `cle1::pdx1::`
(reached a fn). So the failure was edge-level, before any of our code (this also
proves it was NOT the in-app permission layer — requirePermission runs in the
fn). Ruled out, each with a test: stale deploy, build cache (cleared, redeployed
— no change), route-count limit (361 entries, all present + correct locally),
function-count limit (only 5 real fns; 717 .func are symlinks into them), and
`split:true` (deployed commit 6ef0a73 to master — still 404; split only changes
the symlink target, not the form). Local `.vercel/output` was provably correct
every time, which is the key tell: the bug only appears in Vercel's build, not
ours.

### Root cause (confirmed by controlled experiment)
There is a ROOT-LEVEL `api/` directory (`api/ml/infer.py` — a legacy Vercel
Python ONNX inference fn — + `api/requirements.txt`), declared in vercel.json
`functions`. Vercel RESERVES the whole `/api/*` namespace when a root `api/`
dir with serverless fns exists and applies legacy literal-path routing: it
matches `/api/.../<value>` against the literal folder `[id]` and 404s, never
falling through to SvelteKit's regex routes. Static `/api` routes resolve (exact
path match); dynamic PAGE routes are unaffected (not under `/api`). This also
explains local-correct-but-prod-broken: local `npm run build` runs ONLY the
SvelteKit adapter; Vercel ALSO builds the root python fn and merges it, hijacking
`/api/*`. CONFIRMED via preview deploy of branch `test/remove-root-api` (4fcd67c)
which deletes the root `api/` dir — on that preview ALL dynamic endpoints flipped
404 -> 401 (cle1::pdx1::), including `box/files/[id]` which was never split. So
`split` was irrelevant; the root `api/` was the entire problem.

### Impact analysis (wide sweep) — blast radius ~nil
The python fn is effectively DEAD CODE. CV inference moved IN-PROCESS:
cv-bridge.ts header — "used to call an external Python worker over HTTP, now
runs entirely in-process via cv-classifier, no outside service required." Active
path (capture -> runPhaseInference -> cv-bridge.runInference -> cv-classifier
embedImage+predict, classifier weights in the CvProject doc) makes NO python
call. The ONLY code ref to `/api/ml/infer` is the `/api/cv/infer` route (a
"manual one-off inference" endpoint) which itself has ZERO callers (docs-only).
OT-2 daemon treats current 404s as terminal/expired and swallows them — once
endpoints work it just starts succeeding (pure upside). Crons (`/api/cron/*`
SvelteKit routes) unaffected. GH Actions CV training (services/cv-worker +
HTTPS callbacks) never touches root `api/`. SEPARATE pre-existing bug found:
run-inference.ts passed `modelPath` where cv-bridge.runInference expects
`projectId` -> auto-inference always failed "Project not found".

### The fix (PROD-API-NAMESPACE-FIX + CV-INFERENCE-PROJECTID-FIX PRDs)
1. Delete root `api/` dir + remove vercel.json `functions` block -> SvelteKit
   reclaims `/api/*`, all 66 dynamic endpoints resolve.
2. Revert the `split:true` workaround from the 5 endpoints (now unnecessary;
   avoids needless cold-starts).
3. Rewire `/api/cv/infer` to the in-process `runInference` (drops the dead
   python dependency, keeps the manual-inference capability).
4. Fix run-inference.ts to pass `project._id` (not `modelPath`) to runInference.

### NEXT STEP
Push to master -> prod deploy -> re-probe the dynamic endpoints (expect non-404
/ cle1::pdx1::). Then verify OT-2 bridge result/progress POSTs return 200 in the
daemon journal, and run the wax one-button Start Run end to end.

### Prod RESULT: dynamic endpoints fixed (verified ~13:04)
After push, all dynamic /api endpoints flipped 404->401 (cle1::pdx1::). Confirmed.

### Wax page showing the REAGENT protocol — root-caused
The Wax Filling Start Run panel showed "Reagent_Filling...py (reagent-filling)".
Causes: (1) the wax/reagent pages listed ALL of a robot's protocols with NO type
filter, and ProtocolStartPanel defaults to protocols[0]; (2) there is NO wax
protocol on the robots — DB read (opentrons_robots) shows each of B07/R04/B14 has
exactly ONE protocol: Reagent_Filling_GEN7.py, protocolType "reagent-filling".
No wax_filling.py anywhere; protocols are NOT repo files — they live on the robot
+ in robot.protocols[] (read by the mfg pages).

Fix 1 (a3819df): filter each mfg page to its own protocolType (wax-filling /
reagent-filling). Wax page now shows its no-protocol empty state instead of the
reagent default.

### The deeper gap: nothing syncs uploads into robot.protocols[]
The /opentrons Import button POSTs /api/opentrons-lab/robots/[id]/protocols which
forwards the file DIRECTLY to the robot (robotBaseUrl, NOT the bridge) and does
NOT write robot.protocols[]. The deploy endpoint also uses direct HTTP + only
writes OpentronProtocol.deployments[]. No discoverable code populates
robot.protocols[] (the reagent entry came from a now-gone migration/manual step).
So: uploading a protocol never makes it appear on the mfg pages.

Fix 2 (new script scripts/sync-robot-protocols.ts): run on the lab Mac, for each
robot GET /protocols + latest analysis, derive protocolType from the .py filename
(wax*->wax-filling, reagent*->reagent-filling), and $set robot.protocols[]. Dry-run
by default (SYNC_APPLY=1 to write). Guards: skips a robot if unreachable or 0
protocols (never clobbers with empty). UNTESTED against robots from this session
— run dry-run first.

### "fetch failed" on UI upload + "barcode scanning only over local network"
Robots' `ip` field is set to .local mDNS hostnames (OT2CEP20210817R04.local etc.),
not IPs. Upload (and the barcode auto-scan note) go DIRECT to the robot, so they
only work from BIMS on the lab LAN (localhost:5176 + mDNS) — the cloud deploy
can't reach .local => "fetch failed". NOTE: the barcode auto-scan amber note in
the DeckLoadingGrid .svelte is STALE for bridged ops (sweep/deck-scan run via the
daemon now), but it IS still true for UPLOAD (not bridged). The .svelte is frozen
(UI rules) so the copy stays until a UI pass.

DEFERRED (future PRD): bridge the multipart protocol upload (kind:'http' carries
JSON only — needs a new daemon command that base64-decodes the file and POSTs
multipart to localhost:31950/protocols) so uploads work from the cloud deploy.

### Operator path to get the wax protocol live (run on the lab Mac, robots ON)
1. UPLOAD_APPLY=1 npx tsx scripts/upload-local-protocols-to-all-robots.ts
   (pushes every local Opentrons .py — incl. Wax_Filling_GEN7_Cartridge.py — to
   all robots, bundling custom labware so analysis resolves).
2. npx tsx scripts/sync-robot-protocols.ts            (dry run — review)
   SYNC_APPLY=1 npx tsx scripts/sync-robot-protocols.ts   (write robot.protocols[])
3. Wax page now offers Wax_Filling_GEN7_Cartridge.py (wax-filling).


## Session 2026-06-15 — OT2-BRIDGE-3: cloud→robot protocol upload (REFRESHER)

WHERE WE ARE: the prod dynamic-API 404 is fixed (root api/ removed); the wax/reagent
pages filter by protocolType; sync-robot-protocols.ts exists. Open item this session:
uploading a protocol from the CLOUD app failed ("fetch failed") because the Import
endpoint hits the robot DIRECTLY (robotBaseUrl -> .local), which Vercel can't reach,
and upload was never bridged. We bridged it.

WHAT SHIPPED (PRD docs/prds/OT2-BRIDGE-3-CLOUD-PROTOCOL-UPLOAD.md):
- New bridge command kind 'upload_protocol' (Ot2BridgeCommand enum). payload =
  { fileName, fileB64 }.
- Daemon scripts/ot2-bridge.py execute_upload_protocol: base64-decode -> multipart
  POST localhost:31950/protocols -> poll on-robot analysis (<=60s) -> return
  { opentronsProtocolId, analysisStatus, parametersSchema, labwareDefinitions,
  pipettesRequired }. Dispatched from execute_command. (py_compile clean, 3.7-safe.)
- proxy.ts robotUploadProtocol(robot, fileName, bytes): bridge (Vercel) via
  bridgeUpload, or direct (lab LAN) via directUpload (upload + analysis poll).
- Endpoint POST /api/opentrons-lab/robots/[id]/protocols: calls robotUploadProtocol,
  derives protocolType from filename, UPSERTS robot.protocols[] (the array the mfg
  pages read), writes AuditLog. export const config = { maxDuration: 120 } (bridged
  round-trip can exceed 30s). So a cloud Import now lands the protocol AND shows it
  on the Wax page — no lab Mac, no scripts.
- npm run check: 11-error baseline, zero new. npm run build: green.

KNOWN LIMIT: uploads the .py only (no labware bundle). The wax protocol uses custom
GEN7 labware; if on-robot analysis comes back failed/pending the param form won't
populate. The robots already hold the GEN7 labware from earlier script uploads; if a
NEW labware set is ever needed, push it once via upload-local-protocols-to-all-robots.ts.

  (1) don't break on the ACK alone (_decoded_payload); (2) read window 3->5.5s;
  (3) THE real fix: RE-TRIGGER every ~1s across the window (Command Mode = one
  burst per trigger then idle), 0.15s settle before each poke, fresh buffer per
  burst. Confirmed against Waveshare manual V2.1 (command-mode trigger
  7E000801000201ABCD; Single Scanning Time 5s; reread-delay only applies in
  Continuous/Sensing, NOT command mode).
- Homing: home_gantry() skips a re-home if homed <180s ago; deck scan forces one
  home at flow entry; wind-down homes between deck-scan and sweep removed (~2min).
